FILE root_checks/independent_m12_http.py 
 """Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
PORT = 19402
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
CASES = ['[89Z.r]-DFO-daratumumab', '89Z_r-dfo-daratumumab', '89Zr-dfo-daratumumab', '[89Zr]-DFO-daratumumab', '[89Z.r]-DFO-daratumumab, osimertinib', 'daratumumab', 'osimertinib', '[89Z.r]-labelled trastuzumab', '[89Zr]-labelled trastuzumab', 'trastuzumab labelled with [89Zr]', 'trastuzumab', 'rad001c', 'rad001', 'EVEROLIMUS']


def post(endpoint, text):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    with response:
        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
                "response": json.loads(response.read())}

with socket.socket() as sock:
    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
with (OUT / "independent_m12_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
    results = []
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
            try:
                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
                break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.1)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
        for name in CASES:
            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
                results.append(post("ask", f"Who sponsors trials of {name}?"))
        (OUT / "independent_m12_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
        (OUT / "independent_m12_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

FILE root_checks/run_replays.py 
 """Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
rows=[]
for name in names:
    start=time.perf_counter()
    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)


FILE root_checks/fresh_probe.py 
 """Independent small normalization attack matrix, read-only Engine calls."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json
OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
from app import Engine
import query as Q
import textrules as T
engine=Engine()
engine.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
cases=[
 '[89Z.r] trastuzumab','89Z_r trastuzumab','trastuzumab [89Z.r]',
 '[89Zr] trastuzumab','89 Zr trastuzumab','[8.9Zr] trastuzumab',
 'trastuzumab 8 9 Zr','bevacizumab - a - w - w - b',
 'bevacizumab a w w b','bevacizumab - awwb','bevacizumab a.w.w.b',
 'trastuzumab labelled with 89Zr','trastuzumab labeled with 89Zr',
 '89Zr labeled trastuzumab','89Zr conjugated trastuzumab',
 '99MTc trastuzumab','[99MTc] trastuzumab','99mTc trastuzumab',
 'trastuzumab','bevacizumab','osimertinib',
]
rows=[]
for text in cases:
    folded=Q._fold(text)
    raw=engine.resolve(text)
    blocked=engine.kg._resolver_blocked(folded)
    item=engine.kg._link(folded)
    whole=engine.kg._whole_name(folded,blocked,item)
    public=engine.kg.link_entities(text)
    mapped=engine.explore(text)
    rows.append(dict(input=text,key=T.canonical_key(text),words=Q._alnum_words(text),
                     isotope_predicate=T.is_isotope_label(text),raw=raw,blocked=blocked,
                     item=item,whole=whole,public=public,map=mapped))
    print(json.dumps(dict(input=text,center=mapped.get('center'),refused=mapped.get('refused')),ensure_ascii=True),flush=True)
(OUT/'fresh_probe.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
engine.kg.conn.close()

FILE src/query.py
349:         # The resolver's refusals apply to questions too: "Mvasi", "liposomal
350:         # doxorubicin" or "generic version of bevacizumab" must never answer as the
351:         # originator molecule. The text is cut into list items at commas, semicolons
352:         # and listing words ("and", "or", "plus", ...). An item that names a distinct
353:         # product or a tracer anywhere has every drug in it refused: which drug a
354:         # qualifier describes is not guessed, so "osimertinib with generic version of
355:         # bevacizumab" refuses both, while "osimertinib and generic version of
356:         # bevacizumab" keeps osimertinib. Targets and companies in the item still link.
357:         seg: list[int] = []
358:         n = 0
359:         for k, t in enumerate(tokens):
360:             # "and" inside parentheses describes one product ("doxorubicin
361:             # (nanoparticle and liposomal formulation)"); it does not list two
362:             if t.lower() in _JOINERS and not inner[k]:
363:                 n += 1
364:                 seg.append(-1)
365:                 continue
366:             if k and clause[k] != clause[k - 1]:
367:                 n += 1
368:             seg.append(n)
369:         refused: set[int] = {k for k, t in enumerate(tokens)
370:                              if T.is_distinct_product(t) or T.is_radiotracer(t)}
371:         drugs = self._drug_spans(tokens, {k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] < 0}, seg)
372:         dangling = None                 # a qualifier item with no drug, joined by "and"/"or"
373:         prev_end = -1
374:         for sid in sorted(set(seg) - {-1}):
375:             idx = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == sid]
376:             text = " ".join(tokens[k] for k in idx)
377:             joined = (dangling is not None and idx[0] > prev_end + 1
378:                       and all(seg[k] < 0 for k in range(prev_end + 1, idx[0]))
379:                       and clause[idx[0]] == clause[prev_end])
380:             prev_end = idx[-1]
381:             hit = [k for a, b in drugs if seg[a] == sid for k in range(a, b)]
382:             if _qualified([tokens[k] for k in idx]):
383:                 named = any(k in refused and (
384:                     T.canonical_key(tokens[k]) in self.dic["index"]
385:                     or tokens[k].lower() in T.BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | T.REFORMULATION_NAMES)
386:                     for k in idx)
387:                 if not hit and not named:
388:                     # "liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin": a bare
389:                     # qualifier carries over a listing word to the next item's drug; a
390:                     # product named by itself ("Mvasi and bevacizumab") does not
391:                     dangling = text
392:                     continue
393:                 dangling = None
394:             elif joined and hit:
395:                 text = f"{dangling} ... {text}"
396:                 dangling = None
397:             else:
398:                 dangling = None if not joined else dangling
399:                 continue
400:             refused.update(hit)
401:             found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text,
402:                           "matched_text": text,
403:                           "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
404:                                  "the originator molecule"})
405: 
406:         matched: set[int] = set()
407:         for size in range(min(5, len(tokens)), 0, -1):
408:             for i in range(len(tokens) - size + 1):
409:                 if (refused | matched) & set(range(i, i + size)):
410:                     continue
411:                 if size > 1 and (len(set(seg[i:i + size])) > 1 or min(seg[i:i + size]) < 0):
412:                     continue            # a name never spans two list items
413:                 window = " ".join(tokens[i:i + size])
414:                 if len(window) < 3:
415:                     continue
416:                 key = T.canonical_key(window)
417: 
418:                 hits = self.dic["index"].get(key)
419:                 # Several words only make a drug name when the name itself has
420:                 # several words. The key drops spaces, so "is a" read as the
421:                 # abbreviation "isa" (isatuximab) in any question or answer.
422:                 if hits and size > 1 and not re.search(r"[\s\-/]", hits[0]["alias"]):
423:                     hits = None
424:                 if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) == 1:
425:                     did = hits[0]["drug_id"]
426:                     why = self._marked(key, did, tokens, seg, i, i + size)
427:                     if why:
428:                         # the name carries a product suffix or sits beside an isotope:
429:                         # refuse it, never fall back to the bare molecule
430:                         found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": why,
431:                                       "matched_text": why,
432:                                       "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
433:                                              "the originator molecule"})
434:                         refused.update(range(i, i + size))
435:                         continue
436:                     if ("Drug", did) not in seen:
437:                         seen.add(("Drug", did))
438:                         found.append({"type": "Drug", "id": did,
439:                                       "label": self.dic["drugs"][did]["name"],
440:                                       "matched_text": window,

722:         # A biosimilar, a differently-formulated product or a sponsor-specific

764:             res["rules"].append("target_symbol_guard")
765:             out["components"].append(res)
766:             continue

769:         if not hits:

771:                           else T.clean_surface(raw_part)["parentheticals"])
772:             for p in own_parens:
773:                 if T.is_isotope_label(p):
774:                     rules.append("isotope_label_skipped")
775:                     continue
776:                 hits = _lookup(index, p)
777:                 if hits:
778:                     tier = TIER_PAREN
779:                     rules.append("match_on_parenthetical")
780:                     res["component"] = p
781:                     break
782: 
783:         # 3. salt-stripped
784:         if not hits:

786:             if fired and desalted:
787:                 hits = _lookup(index, desalted)
788:                 if hits:
789:                     tier = TIER_SALT

792:         # 3b. an originator's FDA suffix ("cemiplimab-rwlc")
793:         if not hits:

795:             if inn:

기존 테스트는 **340개 모두 통과**했고, 페이지 데모 36개도 이전 라운드와 응답이 일치했습니다. 새 후보에서는 거부 검사 단계들이 모두 통과하며, 지도뿐 아니라 임상·주관기관 질의에도 원형 약물 근거가 각각 2건 반환됩니다. 이전 HEAD에서도 같은 현상이 있어, 새 회귀가 아니라 아직 닫히지 않은 거부 경계로 분류합니다.

audit_evidence_claims\m13\attacks\FINDINGS.md # Round 13 independent refusal attacks

Reviewed HEAD `481100c584ec772cc2332960ba5d3b28ba36ae6f`. **One P1 blocker, M13-B1:** removing an isotope/chelator boundary in a stored tracer alias still draws its originator. Every refusal stage misses it. This is inherited from HEAD^, not a newly introduced regression. The exact M12-B1 inputs are fixed.

## M13-B1 ? isotope/chelator boundary erased inside a matched alias

**Exact HTTP request:** `POST /api/explore`, JSON `{"text":"89ZrDFO-daratumumab"}`.

**Actual:** HTTP 200, centre **DARATUMUMAB / CHEMBL1743007**, matched alias **`89zr-dfo-daratumumab`**, **2 live trials**, and no Refused entity or refusal notice. `Which trials evaluate 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` and `Who sponsors trials of 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` each return **2 originator evidence rows**. The fully glued `89zrdfodaratumumab`, `[89ZrDFO]-daratumumab`, `89Z.rDFO-daratumumab`, `89ZrDFO daratumumab`, and `89-ZrDFO-daratumumab` do likewise. Adding `, osimertinib` still selects DARATUMUMAB. [Full stages](fresh_results.json), [compact matrix](fresh_summary.json), [actual CLI HTTP](fresh_http.json).

**Identity/control evidence:** the candidate and refused `[89Zr]-DFO-daratumumab` have the identical canonical key **`89zrdfodaratumumab`**, which the dictionary maps uniquely to the AACT synonym `89zr-dfo-daratumumab` for DARATUMUMAB. The refused controls `89Zr-dfo-daratumumab`, `[89Zr]-DFO-daratumumab`, and `89Zr-DFOdaratumumab` produce no centre and zero qualified trial/sponsor rows. Bare `daratumumab` remains available. These are alternative spellings accepted as the same stored alias by the application's own key; no external pharmacology assumption is necessary. [Dictionary and predicates](dictionary_evidence.json).

| Stage for `89ZrDFO-daratumumab` | Result |
|---|---|
| Raw resolver | `ALIAS` / CHEMBL1743007, no blocked component |
| Resolver veto | `{}` |
| Item linker | Drug DARATUMUMAB, no Refused |
| Whole-name shortcut | `None`; no previously recorded refusal is overridden |
| Public linker | Drug DARATUMUMAB, no Refused |
| Map | DARATUMUMAB / CHEMBL1743007, 2 trials |
| Trial / sponsor QA | 2 / 2 originator evidence rows |

The source explains this result:

1. Positive lookup removes every non-letter/digit through `canonical_key` (`src/textrules.py:339-346`) and consumes the complete stored alias (`src/query.py:413-426`).
2. `_alnum_words` splits only at separators (`src/query.py:111-113`). The candidate therefore has `['89ZrDFO', 'daratumumab']`, not an `89Zr` word. The new inside-name scan glues whole words but cannot split an alphanumeric word (`src/query.py:258-268`); its six-character limit also stops the first word, whose length is seven. Fully glued input similarly contains no word equal to an isotope.
3. No outside neighbour contains the isotope (`src/query.py:274-290`), because the whole alias was consumed.
4. The isotope-plus-same-drug fallback can isolate `89zr`, but requires remainder `dfodaratumumab` to be a separate dictionary alias of the same drug (`src/query.py:292-296`). That key is absent. `daratumumab` is present.
5. The raw tracer predicate expects a separator or bracket after the isotope and does not recognize the alphanumeric `89ZrDFO` (`src/textrules.py:303-314`, `src/normalize.py:740-745`). `_qualified` likewise returns false (`src/query.py:119-142`). `Engine.explore` selects the surviving positive hit (`src/app.py:87-95`).

**Required outcome:** positive alias normalization must not erase the refused tracer identity when the isotope becomes glued to a chelator or another part of a known tracer alias. Checking only whole alphanumeric words is insufficient; preserving identity must not depend on a nonexistent isotope-stripped remainder alias. No application changes were made.

**Version attribution:** both current and the exact HEAD^ query module (`d9260a1ea544cf7bb80415383667784e0aba146a`) leak these new spellings. The comparison uses the current app, normalization/rules and database with only query.py swapped. It is a same-data module counterfactual, not a reconstruction of an old deployment. [Version-labelled stages](fresh_results.json).

from pathlib import Path
import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
out=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1',PYTHONUTF8='1',PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8',TEMP=str(out/'tmp'),TMP=str(out/'tmp'),TMPDIR=str(out/'tmp'))
rows=[]
for name in ['m7_attacks/fresh_probe.py','m9_attacks/http_probe.py','new_alias_confirmation.py']:
    started=time.perf_counter()
    with (out/(name.replace('/','_')+'.round10.stdout.txt')).open('wb') as log:
        p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(out/name)],env=env,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,timeout=300)
    row=dict(script=name,exit_code=p.returncode,seconds=time.perf_counter()-started)
    rows.append(row)
    (out/'final_replay_execution.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
    print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)

# Round 13: database, UI, demos and independent adjudication

Reviewed HEAD **`481100c584ec772cc2332960ba5d3b28ba36ae6f`**. DB, UI and demo replays establish no new defect in those scopes. Independent adjudication confirms the originator leak **`89ZrDFO-daratumumab` / `89zrdfodaratumumab`**; this inherited unclosed boundary survives HEAD and HEAD^, while the exact M12 reports are fixed. See [adjudication.md](adjudication.md). All worker-created artifacts stay under `audit_evidence_claims/m13/db_ui/`; no application, tracked or earlier-audit file was edited.

## Reproduction coverage

**22 earlier scripts were reused** from m12's cumulative DB/UI suite, including the independent staged adjudicator. Core fixture/assertion logic and historical baseline reads are unchanged. Adaptations are m13 output paths, owned CLI port 19331, and the adjudicator's new candidate/control inputs plus word-boundary diagnostics. [Source/adapted hashes](reuse_manifest.json), [execution](execution.json). No previous output was presented as a fresh run. The historical finding matrix structure is reused, with each disposition validated against the fresh executions below.

- Independent stored-row oracle: **6,469 live centres, 22 relation/direction/type/role combinations, zero errors**. It checks exact live-edge membership, endpoints, stored details, drug counts, totals and ordering. Oracle interval **194.264 s**, overlapping other audit work; it is not an isolated cost measurement. [Oracle](db_validate.json).
- **19/19 DB assertions** pass: retired rows, malformed props, missing sponsor counts, bool/decimal/Infinity scores, unknown roles and 1,001-drug count chunking across three SQL chunks. Mutation fixtures are in-memory only. [Assertions](db_assertions.json).
- **24/24 UI assertions** pass using actual page JavaScript, fresh Engine maps, fixture responses and root's fresh CLI HTTP artifacts. [Assertions](ui_assertions.json), [UI execution](execution_ui_complete.json), [final checks](execution_final.json).
- **All 36 page demos plus six map callbacks pass**. All 36 complete response objects equal m7?m12. Seven QA evidence counts remain **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**. [Full comparisons](demo_previous_comparison.json), [rendered demos](ui_demos_results.json), [callbacks](ui_m3_checks.json).
- **36/36 actual CLI HTTP demo requests** return 200 and equal direct Engine results; served page bytes equal `web/try.html`; owned port **19331 is closed**. [HTTP](demo_http.json), [lifecycle](demo_http_lifecycle.json), [status](worker_status.json).
- Isolated self-loop/unknown-type/retirement fixture passes **4/4** real-handler checks. A self-loop appears once, as outgoing; unknown types/relations retain raw values. [Fixture](self_loop_unknown.json).

UI tests execute actual JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They establish generated markup and state/callback behavior, not browser painting or browser-executed XSS. Original-CLI requests exercise the actual server. No audit server used port 8765.

+"""Assemble only freshly verified m13 evidence into the requested report."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+def adapted(text, prefix):
+    return re.sub(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', lambda m: '](' + prefix + '/' + m.group(1) + ')', text)
+
+def section(text, heading):
+    content = text.split(heading, 1)[1]
+    return content.split('\n## ', 1)[0].strip()
+
+names = (OUT / 'names/FINDINGS.md').read_text(encoding='utf8')
+db = (OUT / 'db_ui/FINDINGS.md').read_text(encoding='utf8')
+cost = (OUT / 'cost/FINDINGS.md').read_text(encoding='utf8')
+name_table = '\n'.join(line for line in names.splitlines() if line.startswith('|'))
+db_table = '\n'.join(line for line in section(db, '## Historical findings: individual dispositions').splitlines() if line.startswith('|'))
+

+UI checks execute actual page JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They verify generated markup and exercised state/callback behavior, not browser painting or browser-executed XSS. Real HTTP checks use unmodified `src/app.py` or its handler with isolated in-memory fixtures. No audit used port 8765. No model was called. +## M13-B1 - P1: removing an isotope/chelator separator bypasses every refusal
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"89ZrDFO-daratumumab"}`.
+
+**Actual:** HTTP 200, centre **DARATUMUMAB / CHEMBL1743007**, matched alias **`89zr-dfo-daratumumab`**, **2 live trials**, no Refused entity or refusal notice. `Which trials evaluate 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` and `Who sponsors trials of 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` each return **2 originator evidence rows**. Fully glued `89zrdfodaratumumab` does the same. Appending `, osimertinib` still selects DARATUMUMAB. [Root HTTP evidence](root_checks/independent_m13_http.json), [separate worker adjudication](db_ui/adjudication.md).
+
+**Control and identity proof:** `[89Zr]-DFO-daratumumab`, `89Zr-dfo-daratumumab` and the repaired `[89Z.r]-DFO-daratumumab` refuse, with no centre and zero qualified trial/sponsor evidence. Bare `daratumumab` works. The leaking spelling and refused control share **canonical key `89zrdfodaratumumab`**, mapped uniquely to the AACT synonym `89zr-dfo-daratumumab` for DARATUMUMAB. Thus the positive lookup recognizes the very stored tracer identity whose refusal is lost; this is not a guess about an unrelated unknown name. [Dictionary evidence](attacks/dictionary_evidence.json).
+
+| Stage for `89ZrDFO-daratumumab` | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | `ALIAS` to CHEMBL1743007; no blocked component |
+| Resolver veto | `{}` |
+| Item linker | Drug DARATUMUMAB; no Refused |
+| Whole-name shortcut | `None`; it does not override an existing refusal |
+| Public linker | Drug DARATUMUMAB; no Refused |
+| Map / trial QA / sponsor QA | Originator centre / 2 evidence rows / 2 evidence rows |
+
+The tested hypothesis is that the latest scan closes split symbols but still relies on input word boundaries. The evidence supports it:
+
+1. Positive lookup deletes separators (`src/textrules.py:339-346`) and matches the complete alias (`src/query.py:413-426`).
+2. `_alnum_words` splits only at non-ASCII-letter/digit separators (`src/query.py:111-113`). The candidate produces `['89ZrDFO', 'daratumumab']`; `89Zr` is not a separate word. The new scan at `src/query.py:258-267` glues complete words but never separates an isotope from an already joined word. The first word has seven characters and exceeds its six-character bound.
+3. All isotope text is inside the consumed alias, so outside-neighbour checks cannot recover it (`src/query.py:273-289`).
+4. The key-prefix fallback isolates `89zr` but requires `dfodaratumumab` to be a same-drug dictionary key (`src/query.py:291-295`). It is absent; only the shorter `daratumumab` key exists. The same failure affects the fully glued alias.
+5. Raw tracer predicates also miss this boundary (`src/textrules.py:303-314`; `src/normalize.py:740-745`); the item guard returns false (`src/query.py:119-141`). With all guards silent, `Engine.explore` selects the surviving hit (`src/app.py:87-95`).
+
+Current and exact HEAD^ (`d9260a1ea544cf7bb80415383667784e0aba146a`) query modules both reproduce the leak against the same current app/rules/database. This identifies an **inherited unclosed boundary**, while exact M12-B1 inputs now refuse. [Current/previous comparison](attacks/fresh_results.json), [independent comparison](db_ui/adjudication_tracer.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** positive alias normalization must preserve refusal of a stored tracer identity even when the isotope is joined to its chelator or another alias component. Refusal must not require an independently stored isotope-stripped remainder. No fix was implemented in this audit.
+
+`A33124I` is an additional example of the same boundary: stored alias `A33 124i` refuses, but the glued spelling maps to ADAKITUG / CHEMBL5314551 because remainder `a33` is not a standalone key. It has zero trial/sponsor evidence; it is corroboration of M13-B1, not a second counted blocker. [Root HTTP](root_checks/independent_m13_http.json), [dictionary evidence](attacks/dictionary_evidence.json).

+"""Preserve tracked bytes; adapt historical audit scripts, never their evidence."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json, subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files', '-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
+initial = {
+    'head': subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+    'tracked_sha256': {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p},
+    'status': subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain'], cwd=ROOT).decode(),
+    'db_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+}
+(OUT / 'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(initial, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+manifest = []
+for sub in ['root_checks', 'cost']:
+    dest = OUT / sub
+    (dest / 'tmp').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for src in sorted((ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m12' / sub).glob('*.py')):
+        if src.name in ('setup.py', 'build_report.py'):
+            continue
+        code = src.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+        changes = []
+        for old, new in [('8917', '19400'), ('8918', '19401'), ('8919', '19402'), ('8914', '19403')]:
+            if old in code:
+                code = code.replace(old, new)
+                changes.append(f'port {old} -> {new}')
+        assert 'audit_evidence_claims/m12/' not in code, src
+        (dest / src.name).write_text(code, encoding='utf8')
+        manifest.append({'source': src.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(), 'copy': (dest/src.name).relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
+                         'sha256': hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), 'changes': changes,
+                         'output_root': '__file__ relative m13; historical inputs unchanged'})
+(OUT / 'reuse_manifest_root.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps({'tracked': len(initial['tracked_sha256']), 'scripts': len(manifest), 'head': initial['head']}))

## M12 repair and retained loss

The exact reported M12 variants have no centre or qualified QA evidence. [Stage evidence](m12_attacks/focused_results.json).

+"""Assemble only freshly verified m13 evidence into the requested report."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+def adapted(text, prefix):
+    return re.sub(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', lambda m: '](' + prefix + '/' + m.group(1) + ')', text)
+
+def section(text, heading):
+    content = text.split(heading, 1)[1]
+    return content.split('\n## ', 1)[0].strip()
+
+names = (OUT / 'names/FINDINGS.md').read_text(encoding='utf8')

+## M13-B1 - P1: removing an isotope/chelator separator bypasses every refusal
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"89ZrDFO-daratumumab"}`.
+
+**Actual:** HTTP 200, centre **DARATUMUMAB / CHEMBL1743007**, matched alias **`89zr-dfo-daratumumab`**, **2 live trials**, no Refused entity or refusal notice. `Which trials evaluate 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` and `Who sponsors trials of 89ZrDFO-daratumumab?` each return **2 originator evidence rows**. Fully glued `89zrdfodaratumumab` does the same. Appending `, osimertinib` still selects DARATUMUMAB. [Root HTTP evidence](root_checks/independent_m13_http.json), [separate worker adjudication](db_ui/adjudication.md).
+
+**Control and identity proof:** `[89Zr]-DFO-daratumumab`, `89Zr-dfo-daratumumab` and the repaired `[89Z.r]-DFO-daratumumab` refuse, with no centre and zero qualified trial/sponsor evidence. Bare `daratumumab` works. The leaking spelling and refused control share **canonical key `89zrdfodaratumumab`**, mapped uniquely to the AACT synonym `89zr-dfo-daratumumab` for DARATUMUMAB. Thus the positive lookup recognizes the very stored tracer identity whose refusal is lost; this is not a guess about an unrelated unknown name. [Dictionary evidence](attacks/dictionary_evidence.json).

+| Stage for `89ZrDFO-daratumumab` | Observed result |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | `ALIAS` to CHEMBL1743007; no blocked component |

+The tested hypothesis is that the latest scan closes split symbols but still relies on input word boundaries. The evidence supports it:
+
+1. Positive lookup deletes separators (`src/textrules.py:339-346`) and matches the complete alias (`src/query.py:413-426`).

The new scan at `src/query.py:258-267` glues complete words but never separates an isotope from an already joined word. The first word has seven characters and exceeds its six-character bound.
+3. All isotope text is inside the consumed alias, so outside-neighbour checks cannot recover it (`src/query.py:273-289`).
+4. The key-prefix fallback isolates `89zr` but requires `dfodaratumumab` to be a same-drug dictionary key (`src/query.py:291-295`). It is absent; only the shorter `daratumumab` key exists. The same failure affects the fully glued alias.
+5. Raw tracer predicates also miss this boundary (`src/textrules.py:303-314`; `src/normalize.py:740-745`); the item guard returns false (`src/query.py:119-141`). With all guards silent, `Engine.explore` selects the surviving hit (`src/app.py:87-95`).

+"""Preserve tracked bytes; adapt historical audit scripts, never their evidence."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json, subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'ls-files', '-z'], cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
+initial = {
+    'head': subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+    'tracked_sha256': {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p},
+    'status': subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain'], cwd=ROOT).decode(),
+    'db_sha256': hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+}
+(OUT / 'integrity_before.json').write_text(json.dumps(initial, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+manifest = []
+for sub in ['root_checks', 'cost']:
+    dest = OUT / sub
+    (dest / 'tmp').mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    for src in sorted((ROOT / 'audit_evidence_claims/m12' / sub).glob('*.py')):
+        if src.name in ('setup.py', 'build_report.py'):
+            continue
+        code = src.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+        changes = []
+        for old, new in [('8917', '19400'), ('8918', '19401'), ('8919', '19402'), ('8914', '19403')]:
+            if old in code:
+                code = code.replace(old, new)
+                changes.append(f'port {old} -> {new}')
+        assert 'audit_evidence_claims/m12/' not in code, src
+        (dest / src.name).write_text(code, encoding='utf8')
+        manifest.append({'source': src.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(), 'copy': (dest/src.name).relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
+                         'sha256': hashlib.sha256(src.read_bytes()).hexdigest(), 'changes': changes,
+                         'output_root': '__file__ relative m13; historical inputs unchanged'})
+(OUT / 'reuse_manifest_root.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps({'tracked': len(initial['tracked_sha256']), 'scripts': len(manifest), 'head': initial['head']}))

exec
